Let's design `project_simplex(v)`:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    x = np.asarray(v)
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]  # assuming sum=1? For projection onto sum=1
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(x - theta, 0)
```
For arbitrary sum? Projection onto simplex sum=1 requires setting x = proj(x - theta); formula with 1 (target sum). More generally p=L project of y onto {p>=0,sum1}: u sorted desc, cssv, find rho where u_rho - (cssv_rho -1)/rho >=0? Standard. Use:
```
dens = np.sort(v)[::-1]
c = np.cumsum(dens)-1
ind = np.nonzero(dens - np.arange(1,n+1)/? )
```
Standard: for simplex sum 1, `theta = (c[rho]) / (rho+1)`, where c=sum dens -1, rho largest with dens_i > c_i/i. Implementation:
```
u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv = np.cumsum(u)
ind = np.arange(1,n+1)
cond = u >= (cssv - 1)/ind
rho = np.max(np.where(cond)[0])
theta = (cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
This projection sums 1 exactly (within fp).

LP implementation details:
```
def lp_direction_step(p_small, dead, iters=...):
    f=p_small; best=f.copy(); best_s=fast_score(f)
    for ...:
       c=conv; M=max c; alpha=softmax(c/tau_lp)
       g=2n*grad_phi
       r=(M+c)/2
       obj = -g
       res = linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=r, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, A_eq? sum=1)
```

Let's design a robust `grad_smooth(p, tau_rel, L=None)`:
```
def score_and_grad(p, tau_rel):
    n=p.size
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M<=0: return 2n, zeros
    tau=max(M*tau_rel, 1e-12)
    z=(c-M)/tau
    z=np.clip(z, -50, 0)
    a=np.exp(z); s=a.sum()
    if not finite: a=zero, argmax=1, s=1
    a/=s
    true=2*n*M
    Wref=a[::-1]
    H=np.fft.irfft(F*np.fft.rfft(Wref,L),L)
    g=2*H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    g = 2*n*g  # objective gradient? Actually g_phi; F=2n phi -> 2n*g
    # clean
    return true, g
```
Check scaling: If objective F=2n*M, and smooth φ= M+τ..., g_phi = dφ/dp, gF =2n*g_phi. The H formula gives d(∑α c_k)/dp = g_phi (which is ~2 conv). So gF=2n*that. Good.

But if we use logit gradient h=p*(gF - mean), mean = dot(p,gF). The step with Adam on z; scale maybe large (2n factor). Since Adam normalizes per coordinate, 2n factor cancels. For projected gradient, gF magnitude O(1)? Wait H entries ~a~1/n; g_phi ~2/n; times 2n ~2. Yes.

`project_simplex` should use y and gF not multiplied by 2n? Already O(1). Good.

Adam with logit uses h; if no-p, h magnitude maybe O(1), Adam cancels. Step lr in z units. We can set lr 0.1-0.5. Need choose.

Projected gradient optimizer details:
```
def proj_grad(parent, duration, rng, tau_start=0.2, tau_end=0.05, lr=0.05, momentum=0.9, restart_every=...):
    p=normalize; L=...
    prev=p.copy(); y=p.copy()
    best=p,score
    step=0; current_lr=lr; current_tau=tau_start; no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
       s,g=score_and_grad(p,current_tau,L)
       if s<best: update
       # Nesterov y = p + momentum*(p - prev)
       y = p + momentum*(p - prev)
       # optional line search: for alpha in [current_lr, current_lr/2,...]
       new = project_simplex(y - current_lr * g)
       # if score worse than score_y? compute new_s
       new_s=fast_score(new)
       if not finite or new_s > s*(1+1e-7): # backtrack
           for f in [0.5,0.25,0.125]:
              cand=project_simplex(y - current_lr*f*g)
              cs=fast_score(cand)
              if cs < s: new=cand; new_s=cs; break
           else:
              # skip; reduce lr
              current_lr*=0.7
              prev=p; continue
       p=new; prev old?; 
       # slowly decay lr? current_lr *= 0.999
```
Need update prev: In Nesterov, set prev_p = p_old before p_new. Actually maintain p_old. Use:
```
p_old = p
p = new
# v = p - p_old? For Nesterov: y = p_old + mu*(p_old - p_prev)
```
We can maintain prev = p_old. At start prev=p. At end set prev = p_old (not new?) Standard FISTA: y_k = x_k + (t_k-1)/(t_{k+1})(x_k - x_{k-1}). Simpler momentum: v = mu*v + (x_k - x_{k-1}); y=x_k+v; x_{k+1}=project(y-lr g(x_k)); prev=x_k. Let's implement:
```
v=0
while:
 y = p + v
 grad at p
 new=project(y-lr*g)
 v_new = mu*(new - p)? Or before? Heavy ball: v = mu*v + (new - y?);
```
Simpler: use plain projected gradient with strong momentum? We can use v = momentum*(p - prev); y = p + v; new=project(y - lr*g); prev=p; p=new. Good.

```
def softmax(z):
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -60, 0)
    e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s==0 e=ones; return e/s
```

```
def better(p,s,best_p,best_s):
   if p is None or not finite(s): return best_p,best_s
   if best_p is None or s < best_s: return p.copy(), s
   return best_p,best_s
```

Projected gradient optimizer: Use `project_simplex` formula. Need ensure if v has NaN, reset.
```
def projected_gradient(parent, deadline, rng, lr=0.03, momentum=0.9, tau_start=0.2, tau_end=0.05):
    p=normalize; best=...
    prev=p.copy(); v=np.zeros(n)
    step=0; current_lr=lr; current_tau=tau_start; no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
       score,g = score_and_grad(p,current_tau,L)
       if score<best: update
       v = momentum*v + (p - prev)
       y = p + v
       cand = project_simplex(y - current_lr*g)
       cand_s=fast_score(cand)
       if not finite: cand=project_simplex(y - current_lr*0.5*g); ...
       accept if cand_s < score or random? Use if cand_s < score*1.0 else backtrack.
       if accept: prev=p; p=cand; if not improving best maybe continue
       else: current_lr *=0.8; no_improve++ ; if no_improve>... restart
       v decay if no? 
```

Now, let's think of correctness of `project_simplex` for large n. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n = v.size
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * ind >= cssv - 1.0
    if not np.any(cond): rho = n-1
    else: rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(cond)[0]))
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    out /= out.sum()  # correct sum due roundoff
    return out
```
For v all zeros, u=0, css=0, cond 0>=-1 true all, rho n-1, theta=(0-1)/n=-1/n, out=max(theta)=1/n uniform. Good.

`resize_sequence` with np.interp requires xp strictly increasing; if original length 1, handle. For length 2+ okay.

`score_and_grad` uses p sum1; if not, normalize.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
```

Random smooth function:
```
def random_smooth(N, rng, K=6):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,N)
    z = rng.normal(0,1.0) + rng.normal(0,0.5)*x + rng.normal(0,0.3)*x*x
    for k in range(1, K+1):
       z += rng.normal(0, 0.4/(k+1)*(1 if k%2==0 else 1?)) * np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x)
       z += rng.normal(0, 0.4/(k+1)) * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x)
    z = z - z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -8, 8)
    return normalize_positive(np.exp(z))
```
Asymmetric arises from linear x term. Generate e.g., 30. For N=8192, each exp O(n). 30*8192=245k, trivial. But random smooth may produce endpoints with values not too extreme due exp clip. Good.

Lowpass random:
```
def random_lowpass(N,rng):
   z = rng.normal(size=N)
   Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
   cut = np.random.choice([1,2,3,4,5]) maybe relative to N? For smooth, cut few frequencies.
   mask = np.zeros_like(Z); mask[:cut]=1; Z*=mask
   z = np.fft.irfft(Z, N)
   z = z - z.mean(); clip; p=exp(z)
```
This can create asymmetric smooth shapes. Include.

```
def safe_exp_norm(z):
    z = np.asarray(z,float); z = np.where(finite,z,0); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-10,10); p=np.exp(z); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return None; return p/s
```
But top-level helper? Could be within make? No closures? Helper top-level `dens_from_z`.
```
def dens_from_z(z):
   z = np.asarray(z,float)
   z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0)
   z = z - np.mean(z)
   z = np.clip(z, -10, 10)
   e = np.exp(z)
   s = e.sum()
   if s <= 0 or not finite: return None
   return e/s
```

```
def adam_logit(parent, deadline, rng, use_p=False, tau_start=0.3, tau_end=0.02, lr=0.08, clip_z=40.0):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None: return None, np.inf
    n=p.size; L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z = np.where(finite,z,-40); z=z-z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-clip_z,clip_z)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); step=0
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    best_p=p.copy(); best_s=fast_score(p)
    cur_tau=float(tau_start); cur_lr=float(lr); no_improve=0; noise_amp=0.2
    print_interval = 2000 if n<=4096 else 10000
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        true_s, g = score_and_grad(p, cur_tau, L)
        if not finite(true_s): break
        if true_s < best_s - 1e-12:
             best_s=true_s; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
        else: no_improve += 1
        if step % 2000 == 0:
             cur_tau = max(float(tau_end), cur_tau*0.995)
        # h
        dg = float(np.dot(p,g))
        h = g - dg
        if use_p: h = p * h
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        if step > 0 and step % 5000 == 0:
             lr? maybe decrease slowly: cur_lr=max(0.01, cur_lr*0.98)
        bias_step? Need initialize bias count as step+1 after update.
        m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*h
        v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*h*h
        b1=1-beta1**(step+1); b2=1-beta2**(step+1)
        update = (m/b1)/(np.sqrt(v/b2)+eps)
        # scale update: maybe if use_p updates smaller, scale inversely? Use `scale = cur_lr * (0.5 if use_p? no)`
        # Clip per-coordinate update to ±cur_lr? In Adam, alpha is cur_lr. Use max_step = 2.0? Let's set max_step = min(2.0, 10*cur_lr)? Maybe too.
        max_step = 2.0
        if cur_lr < max_step: max_step = cur_lr*2? Hmm.
```
In standard Adam, update = alpha * m/(sqrtv+eps) where alpha is learning rate (max step). If alpha=0.08, per-coord step ≤0.08. Fine. We don't need clip? Because m/(sqrtv+eps) ~1, step ~0.08. But if v small initially, step maybe >1. Use clip to `alpha*10`? Standard not clipped. Could clip to `max_step = max(1.0, 10*cur_lr)` to prevent first step huge? In Adam, initial v small, m/(sqrtv) can be ~1 if h nonzero, so step=alpha≤1. So okay. Use `z -= cur_lr * update`.
```
        z = z - cur_lr * update
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0)
        z = np.clip(z, -clip_z, clip_z)
        p = softmax(z)
        if not all finite p: break
        step +=1
        if no_improve > 4000:
            # restart
            zz = np.log(np.maximum(best_p,1e-300)); ... + rng.normal(0,noise_amp,n)
            z = clip; m*=0.1; v*=0.1; no_improve=0; cur_tau=max(tau_end, cur_tau*1.8); noise_amp=min(1.0, noise_amp*1.2); cur_lr=max(0.02, cur_lr*0.9)
        if step % print_interval ==0: print(...)
    return best_p,best_s
```
Annealing cur_lr: perhaps reduce slowly every 20k to 0.01. But if no improve, restart.

Potential problem: If use_p=True, h entries ~1/n, m and v ~1/n^2? update m/sqrtv still O(1), step alpha normal. So use_p not under-scaled due Adam. Good.

`projected_gradient`:
```
def projected_gradient(parent, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, momentum=0.9, tau_start=0.2, tau_end=0.05):
    p=normalize...
    best...
    prev=p.copy(); v=np.zeros(n); step=0; cur_lr=lr; cur_tau=tau_start; no_improve=0; last_best_time=time
    while time<deadline:
       s,g=score_and_grad(p,cur_tau,L)
       if finite and s<best: update
       v = momentum * v + (p - prev)
       y = p + v
       cand = project_simplex(y - cur_lr*g)
       cs = fast_score(cand)
       accepted = False
       if finite and cs < s - 1e-13:
           cand_b, cs_b = cand, cs
           accepted=True
       else:
           for frac in (0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
               cand2=project_simplex(y - cur_lr*frac*g)
               cs2=fast_score(cand2)
               if finite and cs2 < s - 1e-13:
                   cand=cand2; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           prev=p; p=cand
           # reduce lr slowly
           cur_lr = max(0.005, cur_lr*0.999)
       else:
           # shrink momentum and lr, stay
           v *= 0.5
           cur_lr *= 0.8
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 5000:
              # restart from best with small noise
              p = normalize_positive(best_p + rng.normal(0,1e-4,n))
              prev=p.copy(); v=0; cur_lr=max(0.02, cur_lr*1.2); no_improve=0
           continue
       step +=1
       if step%20000==0: print
    return best_p,best_s
```

```
def dens_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0: return None
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m = float(np.mean(z))
    if np.isfinite(m): z = z - m
    z = np.clip(z, -10.0, 10.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return None
    return e/s
```

```
import time

    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    HAVE_LINPROG = False

def next_pow2(x): ...
def normalize_positive(a): ...
def fast_score(a): ...
def softmax_from_z(z): ...
def score_grad(p, tau_rel, L=None): ...
def project_simplex(v): ...
def resize_sequence(seq, m): ...
def better_update(p,s,best_p,best_s): ...
def dens_from_z(z): ...
def make_candidates(N, rng): ...
def adam_logit(parent, deadline, rng, use_p=False, tau_start=0.3, tau_end=0.02, lr=0.08, clip_z=40.0, print_interval=20000): ...
def projected_gradient(parent, deadline, rng, lr=0.04, momentum=0.9, tau_start=0.2, tau_end=0.05, print_interval=20000): ...
def lp_escape(parent, deadline, rng, n_lp=1024): ...
```

Let's detail `score_grad`:
```
def score_grad(p, tau_rel, L=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0: return np.inf, p, 0.0, None
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return np.inf, p,0,None
    if s != 1.0: p = p / s
    if L is None: L = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: return np.inf, c, M, np.zeros(n)
    tau = max(float(M) * max(1e-6, float(tau_rel)), 1e-12)
    z = (c - M) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0:
       w = np.zeros_like(c); imax=int(np.argmax(c)); w[imax]=1.0; sw=1.0
    w = w / sw
    Wref = w[::-1]
    H = np.fft.irfft(F * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
    g = 2.0 * H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    score = float(2.0 * n * M)
    g *= float(2.0 * n)
    return score, c, M, g
```
Check if tau_rel very small; tau=M*tau_rel. If M 1e-4, tau_rel0.02 ->2e-6. z min maybe -50; okay.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.where(finite,a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return inf
    p=a/s
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=rfft(p,L); c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]; c=max(0); M=max(c)
    if M<=0: return inf
    return 2nM
```
Could reuse score_grad but fast simpler.

`softmax_from_z`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -40.0)
    z=z - float(np.max(z))
    z=np.clip(z,-60.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0: return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
    return e/s
```

`adam_logit` details: Need `bias_step` count starts at 0. Use step variable after update. Let's code:
```
bias_step=0
while time < deadline:
   s,c,M,g = score_grad(p, cur_tau, L)
   if not finite(s): break
   ...
   bias_step += 1
   m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*h
   v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(h*h)
   b1=1-beta1**bias_step
   b2=1-beta2**bias_step
   update = (m/b1)/(np.sqrt(v/b2)+1e-8)
   z = z - cur_lr * update
   ...
   step += 1
```

Let's implement `project_simplex` robust:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n = v.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * ind >= (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        rho = n - 1
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(cond)[0]))
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.ones(n)/n
    return out / s
```
For n=1, works: u=[v], cssv=[v], cond v*1>=v-1 true, rho0, theta=(v-1), out max(v-(v-1),0)=1, sum1. Good.

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p=normalize_positive(seq)
    m=int(m)
    if m<=0: return None
    if p is None: return np.ones(m)/m
    n=p.size
    if n==m: return p.copy()
    if n==1: return np.ones(m)/m
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
    return out/s
```
For sparse, interp creates positive small ramps; okay.

`better_update`:
```
def better_update(p,s,best_p,best_s):
    if p is None or not np.isfinite(s): return best_p,best_s
    if best_p is None or s < best_s:
        return np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(s)
    return best_p,best_s
```

```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x <= 1: return 1
```

```python
"""
Non-negative step-function coefficient search.

The search minimizes
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
by working on normalized nonnegative coefficients p (sum p = 1).

Main tools:
- FFT-based exact score and smooth-max/log-sum-exp gradients.
- Multiple diverse seeds: previous construction, low-degree smooth densities,
  low-pass random fields, geometric/exponential profiles, mixture/geometric-mean
  blends of the best seeds.
- Several optimizers:
    * logit-space Adam (both "raw" gradient and softmax-proper gradient variant)
    * projected gradient / heavy-ball on the simplex
    * a small LP direction finder using the first-order convex envelope
      c_k(x*p) <= (M + c_k)/2.
- Final validation with the actual evaluator on a few length variants.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LINPROG = False


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    p = a / s
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -40.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, z.size)
    return e / s


def score_grad(p, tau_rel, L=None):
    """
    Returns (true_score, convolution_coefficients, max_coefficient, gradient).
    Gradient is for the smooth objective 2*n*softplus(logsumexp(c/tau)).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf, p, 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if s != 1.0:
        p = p / s
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, c, M, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    tau = max(float(M) * max(1e-6, float(tau_rel)), 1e-12)
    z = (c - M) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        imax = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[imax] = 1.0
        sw = 1.0
    w = w / sw

    Wref = w[::-1]
    H = np.fft.irfft(F * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
    g = 2.0 * H[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g *= float(2.0 * n)

    score = float(2.0 * n * M)
    return score, c, M, g


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * ind >= (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        rho = n - 1
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(cond)[0]))
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        out = out / s
    return out


def better_update(p, s, best_p, best_s):
    if p is None or not np.isfinite(s):
        return best_p, best_s
    if best_p is None or s < best_s:
        return np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(s)
    return best_p, best_s


def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p = normalize_positive(seq)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    if p is None:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    n = p.size
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        out = out / s
    return out


def dens_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return None
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    mz = float(np.mean(z))
    if np.isfinite(mz):
        z = z - mz
    z = np.clip(z, -10.0, 10.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return e / s


def make_candidates(N, rng):
    out = []
    if N <= 0:
        return out

    u = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    out.append(u)

    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        hs = np.where(np.isfinite(hs), hs, 0.0)
        hs = np.maximum(hs, 0.0)
        if hs.size > 0 and float(hs.sum()) > 1e-12:
            hr = resize_sequence(hs, N)
            if hr is not None:
                out.append(hr)
                out.append(hr[::-1])
    except Exception:
        pass

    if N == 1:
        return out

    idx = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64)
    x = idx / float(N - 1)

    exprs = (
        -x,
        x,
        -2.0 * x,
        2.0 * x,
        -0.7 * x,
        0.7 * x,
        -x * x,
        x * x,
        -2.0 * x + x * x,
        2.0 * x - x * x,
        -1.0 * x + 0.5 * x * x,
        1.0 * x - 0.5 * x * x,
        -3.0 * x + 1.5 * x * x,
        3.0 * x - 1.5 * x * x,
        -x + x * x * x,
        x - x * x * x,
    )
    for ze in exprs:
        p = dens_from_z(ze)
        if p is not None:
            out.append(p)
            out.append(p[::-1])

    for _ in range(32):
        z = (
            rng.normal(0.0, 0.8)
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.9) * x
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.55) * x * x
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25) * x * x * x
        )
        for k in range(1, 7):
            amp = 0.35 / float(k + 1)
            z += amp * rng.normal() * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x)
            z += amp * rng.normal() * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x)
        p = dens_from_z(z)
        if p is not None:
            out.append(p)
            out.append(p[::-1])

    for _ in range(12):
        z0 = rng.normal(size=N)
        Z = np.fft.rfft(z0)
        ml = len(Z)
        cut = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, 8)))
        if cut >= ml:
            cut = max(1, ml - 1)
        mask = np.ones_like(Z, dtype=np.float64)
        mask[cut:] = 0.0
        Z = Z * mask
        z = np.fft.irfft(Z, N)
        z = z + 0.45 * rng.normal() * x
        p = dens_from_z(z)
        if p is not None:
            out.append(p)
            out.append(p[::-1])

    return out


def adam_logit(parent, deadline, rng, use_p=False,
               tau_start=0.30, tau_end=0.02, lr=0.08, clip_z=40.0,
               print_interval=20000):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -40.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -clip_z, clip_z)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    bias_step = 0
    step = 0

    cur_tau = float(tau_start)
    cur_lr = float(lr)
    noise_amp = 0.25

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        true_s, _, _, g = score_grad(p, cur_tau, L)
        if not np.isfinite(true_s):
            break

        if true_s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = float(true_s)
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if step % 5000 == 0:
            cur_tau = max(float(tau_end), cur_tau * 0.990)
        if step % 20000 == 0 and step > 0:
            cur_lr = max(0.01, cur_lr * 0.985)

        dg = float(np.dot(p, g))
        h = g - dg
        if use_p:
            h = p * h
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

        bias_step += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * h
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (h * h)
        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** bias_step
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** bias_step
        update = (m / b1) / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps)

        z = z - cur_lr * update
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z = np.clip(z, -clip_z, clip_z)
        p = softmax_from_z(z)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            break

        step += 1

        if no_improve > 4000:
            zz = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300))
            zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, -40.0)
            zz = zz + rng.normal(0.0, noise_amp, n)
            zz = zz - float(np.mean(zz))
            z = np.clip(zz, -clip_z, clip_z)
            m *= 0.1
            v *= 0.1
            bias_step = 0
            no_improve = 0
            cur_tau = max(float(tau_end), cur_tau * 1.8)
            cur_lr = max(0.02, cur_lr * 0.85)
            noise_amp = min(1.2, noise_amp * 1.25)

        if step % max(1, int(print_interval)) == 0:
            print("adam use_p={} step={} true={:.6f} best={:.6f}".format(
                int(use_p), step, true_s, best_s))

    return best_p, best_s


def projected_gradient(parent, deadline, rng,
                       lr=0.04, momentum=0.90,
                       tau_start=0.20, tau_end=0.05,
                       print_interval=20000):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    prev = p.copy()
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cur_lr = float(lr)
    cur_tau = float(tau_start)
    no_improve = 0
    step = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        s, _, _, g = score_grad(p, cur_tau, L)
        if not np.isfinite(s):
            break

        if s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = float(s)
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        old_p = p
        vel = momentum * vel + (old_p - prev)
        vn = float(np.max(np.abs(vel)))
        if vn > 1e-12:
            vel *= min(1.0, 0.08 / vn)
        y = p + vel

        cand = project_simplex(y - cur_lr * g)
        cs = fast_score(cand)
        accepted = False
        accepted_cand = cand

        if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < s - 1e-13:
            accepted = True
        else:
            for frac in (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
                cand2 = project_simplex(y - cur_lr * frac * g)
                cs2 = fast_score(cand2)
                if np.isfinite(cs2) and cs2 < s - 1e-13:
                    accepted = True
                    accepted_cand = cand2
                    break

        if accepted:
            p = accepted_cand
            prev = old_p
            cur_tau = max(float(tau_end), cur_tau * 0.995)
            step += 1
        else:
            vel *= 0.35
            cur_lr *= 0.75
            prev = p
            no_improve += 2

            if no_improve > 3000:
                nz = 1.0 / max(1, n)
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 10.0 * nz, n)
                p = normalize_positive(best_p + noise)
                if p is None:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                prev = p.copy()
                vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                cur_lr = max(0.02, cur_lr * 1.3)
                no_improve = 0

        if step % max(1, int(print_interval)) == 0:
            print("projgrad step={} true={:.6f} best={:.6f}".format(step, s, best_s))

    return best_p, best_s


def lp_escape(parent, deadline, rng, n_lp=1024):
    if not HAVE_LINPROG:
        return None, np.inf

    f = resize_sequence(parent, n_lp)
    if f is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = f.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best_p = f.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(f)
    no_improve = 0
    A_eq = np.ones((1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    for it in range(2000):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        _, c, M, g = score_grad(f, 0.10, L)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        rhs = 0.5 * (M + c)
        rhs = np.maximum(rhs, c + 1e-12)
        rhs = rhs + 1e-10 * float(M) + 1e-12

        B = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            B[i:i + n, i] = f

        obj = -g
        obj = obj + 1e-9 * rng.normal(size=n)

        try:
            res = linprog(
                obj,
                A_ub=B,
                b_ub=rhs,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs"
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        sx = float(x.sum())
        if sx <= 1e-12:
            break
        x = x / sx

        improved = False
        for t in (0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * f + t * x
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            sc = float(cand.sum())
            if sc <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = cand / sc
            sc = fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s = float(sc)
                best_p = cand.copy()
                f = cand
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 7:
                break

        if (it + 1) % 50 == 0:
            print("lp step={} best={:.6f}".format(it + 1, best_s))

    return best_p, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    raw = max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    if raw > 1500.0:
        final_buffer = 100.0
    elif raw > 600.0:
        final_buffer = 60.0
    elif raw > 200.0:
        final_buffer = 35.0
    else:
        final_buffer = 15.0

    main_deadline = t0 + max(20.0, raw - final_buffer)
    final_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, raw - 2.0)

    if raw < 180.0:
        N = 1024
    elif raw < 700.0:
        N = 4096
    else:
        N = 8192

    candidates = make_candidates(N, rng)

    scored = []
    for p in candidates:
        pn = normalize_positive(p)
        if pn is None:
            continue
        s = fast_score(pn)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((float(s), len(scored), pn.copy()))
    scored.sort()

    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf
    for s, _, p in scored:
        best_p, best_s = better_update(p, s, best_p, best_s)
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
        best_s = 2.0

    top = [(s, p) for s, _, p in scored[:8]]

    if len(top) >= 2:
        extra = []
        max_pair = min(5, len(top))
        for ii in range(max_pair):
            p1 = top[ii][1]
            for jj in range(ii + 1, max_pair):
                p2 = top[jj][1]
                for w in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
                    mix = (1.0 - w) * p1 + w * p2
                    pm = normalize_positive(mix)
                    if pm is not None:
                        extra.append(pm)
                gm = np.sqrt(np.maximum(p1, 0.0) * np.maximum(p2, 0.0))
                pg = normalize_positive(gm)
                if pg is not None:
                    extra.append(pg)
        for p in extra:
            s = fast_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                scored.append((float(s), len(scored), p.copy()))
                best_p, best_s = better_update(p, s, best_p, best_s)
        scored.sort()
        top = [(s, p) for s, _, p in scored[:8]]

    # Short explorations from several good seeds.
    if raw > 900.0 and (main_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 320.0:
        exp_budget = min(300.0, (main_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.22)
        if exp_budget > 70.0:
            n_exp = min(3, len(top))
            per = exp_budget / float(max(1, n_exp))
            for idx in range(n_exp):
                if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline:
                    break
                rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
                tl = time.monotonic() + min(per, rem)
                seedp = top[idx][1]
                try:
                    if idx == 0:
                        p2, s2 = adam_logit(seedp, tl, rng, use_p=False,
                                            tau_start=0.35, tau_end=0.05, lr=0.10)
                    elif idx == 1:
                        p2, s2 = projected_gradient(seedp, tl, rng,
                                                    lr=0.035, momentum=0.90,
                                                    tau_start=0.25, tau_end=0.07)
                    else:
                        p2, s2 = adam_logit(seedp, tl, rng, use_p=True,
                                            tau_start=0.25, tau_end=0.04, lr=0.06)
                    best_p, best_s = better_update(p2, s2, best_p, best_s)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    best_p = resize_sequence(best_p, N)

    # Long optimization phases.
    rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 130.0:
        dur = min(rem * 0.38, 680.0)
        tl = time.monotonic() + dur
        try:
            p2, s2 = adam_logit(best_p, tl, rng, use_p=False,
                                tau_start=0.30, tau_end=0.035, lr=0.085)
            best_p, best_s = better_update(p2, s2, best_p, best_s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0:
        dur = min(rem * 0.20, 260.0)
        tl = time.monotonic() + dur
        try:
            p2, s2 = projected_gradient(best_p, tl, rng,
                                        lr=0.038, momentum=0.88,
                                        tau_start=0.18, tau_end=0.055)
            best_p, best_s = better_update(p2, s2, best_p, best_s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0:
        dur = min(rem * 0.34, 480.0)
        tl = time.monotonic() + dur
        try:
            p2, s2 = adam_logit(best_p, tl, rng, use_p=True,
                                tau_start=0.18, tau_end=0.02, lr=0.055)
            best_p, best_s = better_update(p2, s2, best_p, best_s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # High-noise restart from one of the ranked seeds or the incumbent.
    rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        start = best_p
        if len(top) > 1:
            k = int(rng.integers(0, len(top)))
            start = top[k][1]
        dur = min(rem * 0.18, 140.0)
        tl = time.monotonic() + dur
        try:
            p2, s2 = adam_logit(start, tl, rng, use_p=False,
                                tau_start=0.55, tau_end=0.08, lr=0.13)
            best_p, best_s = better_update(p2, s2, best_p, best_s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # LP escape on a moderate dimension.
    rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if HAVE_LINPROG and rem > 80.0:
        dur = min(rem * 0.13, 90.0)
        tl = time.monotonic() + dur
        try:
            lp_p, lp_s = lp_escape(best_p, tl, rng, n_lp=1024)
            if lp_p is not None:
                lp_p = resize_sequence(lp_p, N)
                best_p, best_s = better_update(lp_p, lp_s, best_p, best_s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final polish.
    rem = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        dur = max(10.0, rem * 0.75)
        tl = time.monotonic() + dur
        try:
            p2, s2 = adam_logit(best_p, tl, rng, use_p=False,
                                tau_start=0.09, tau_end=0.008, lr=0.035)
            best_p, best_s = better_update(p2, s2, best_p, best_s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final validation candidates across a few lengths.
    final_raw = []
    if best_p is not None:
        final_raw.append(best_p.copy())

    for m in (4096, 2048):
        if best_p is not None and m != N:
            rp = resize_sequence(best_p, m)
            if rp is not None:
                final_raw.append(rp)

    if best_p is not None and raw > 1400.0:
        big = resize_sequence(best_p, 16384)
        if big is not None:
            final_raw.append(big)

    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        hs = np.where(np.isfinite(hs), hs, 0.0)
        hs = np.maximum(hs, 0.0)
        if hs.size > 0 and float(hs.sum()) > 1e-12:
            final_raw.append(hs)
            for m in (4096, 2048):
                rp = resize_sequence(hs, m)
                if rp is not None:
                    final_raw.append(rp)
    except Exception:
        pass

    final_raw.append(np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0)

    final_scored = []
    for p in final_raw:
        pn = normalize_positive(p)
        if pn is None:
            continue
        s = fast_score(pn)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            final_scored.append((float(s), len(final_scored), pn.copy()))
    final_scored.sort()

    answer = None
    long_evaluated = 0
    for s, _, p in final_scored[:5]:
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 8.0:
            break
        n_len = p.size
        if n_len > 12000:
            if long_evaluated >= 1:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 25.0:
                continue
            long_evaluated += 1

        lst = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)]
        if not lst:
            continue
        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        except Exception:
            ev = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            answer = lst

    if answer is None:
        if final_scored:
            p = final_scored[0][2]
            answer = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)]
        else:
            answer = [1.0 / 2048.0] * 2048

    return answer
```
